# TFRKP Hong Nak: tfrkp 원본 덮어쓰기 (korea.115 후보 선택, korea.292 대선 결과만 변경)
add_namespace = korea
add_namespace = koryhg # 윤핵관용 이벤트
add_namespace = korea_hidden

country_event = {
	id = korea.1
	title = korea.1.t
	desc = korea.1.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_kor_start_1
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event korea.1 option korea.1.a"
		name = korea.1.a
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.2
	title = korea.2.t
	desc = korea.2.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_kor_start_2
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_crisis
			activate_mission = KOR_coronavirus_infection_mission
			activate_mission = KOR_coronavirus_death_mission
			KOR_coronavirus_setup_effect = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event korea.2 option korea.2.a"
		name = korea.2.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_coronavirus_alert_tooltip
		hidden_effect = {
			complete_national_focus = KOR_covid_crisis_materialized
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.3
	title = korea.3.t
	desc = korea.3.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_kor_vaccine

	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		check_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_var = 100 }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_researched
			set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_infection_change = -15 }
			remove_mission = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_mission
			remove_mission = KOR_coronavirus_infection_mission
			activate_mission = KOR_coronavirus_infection_cure_mission
			set_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_fatality = 0.01 }
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var_temp = -20 }
			KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var_change_effect = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event korea.3 option korea.3.a"
		name = korea.3.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			remove_mission = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_mission
			remove_mission = KOR_coronavirus_infection_mission
			activate_mission = KOR_coronavirus_infection_cure_mission
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var_temp = -20 }
			KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_var_change_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.4
	title = korea.4.t
	desc = korea.4.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_covid_end

	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		AND = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_vaccine_researched
			check_variable = { KOR.KOR_coronavirus_infection_var = 0 }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_coronavirus_crisis
			KOR = {
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_coronavirus_dynamic }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = KOR_coronavirus_dissatisfaction_dynamic }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event korea.4 option korea.4.a"
		name = korea.4.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_coronavirus_result_tooltip
		set_variable = { KOR_achieve_count_corona_var = KOR_coronavirus_deaths_number }
		if = {
			limit = {			
				check_variable = {  
					var = KOR_achieve_count_corona_var
					value = 5
					compare = less_than_or_equals
				}
			}	
			set_country_flag = KOR_corona_under_50k_flag
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_corona_pandemic_end_flag
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.11 #양안전쟁 패배
	title = korea.11.t
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					KOR_is_lee_path = yes
					has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_lee_path }
			}
		}
		text = korea.11.desc
	}	
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			KOR_is_lee_path = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag }
		}
		text = korea.11.nothing.desc
	}	
	picture = GFX_report_event_kor_china_defeat
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					KOR_is_yoon_path = yes
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = authoritarian_democrat
					elections_allowed = yes
				}
				KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char = {
					set_portraits = {
						civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_defeat_civilian_large }
					}
					add_country_leader_role = {
						country_leader = {
							desc = KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char_defeat_desc
							traits = { kor_failed_yoon_tratis }
							ideology = judicial_authoritarianism
						}
						promote_leader = yes
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = KOR_yoon_taiwan_war_defeat
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = authoritarian_democrat
					long_name = KOR_authoritarian_democrat_party_PPP_long
					name = KOR_authoritarian_democrat_party_PPP
				}
				set_temp_variable = { ruling_party_wing_var_temp = token:conservative }
				add_ruling_party_wing = yes
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_conservative_pop = party_popularity@conservative }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_conservative_second_pop = party_popularity@conservative }
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_conservative_pop = 0.8 }
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_conservative_second_pop = 0.2 }
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = authoritarian_democrat
					popularity = KOR_conservative_pop
				}
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_conservative_pop = -1 }
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = conservative
					popularity = KOR_conservative_pop
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = conservative
					popularity = KOR_conservative_second_pop
				}
				remove_ideas = KOR_dispatch_to_taiwan_idea
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_youngsan }
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youngsan
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_socity }
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youn_socity
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_police_department }
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youn_police_department
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_father
							has_completed_focus = KOR_koreajapan
						}
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_koreajapan
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_party_control
							has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_populace_first
							has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_party_together
						}
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youn_party_control
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					KOR_is_lee_path = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_defeat
				KOR_lee_jae_myung_char = {
					set_portraits = {
						civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_KOR_lee_jae_myung_char_nothing_civilian_large }
					}
					swap_country_leader_traits = {
						remove = kor_lee_jaming_2022_traits
						add = kor_lee_jaming_2022_fail_traits
						ideology = korean_social_liberal
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
					}
					set_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_defeat_supported
					remove_ideas = KOR_lee_load_of_war_idea
				}
				else = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_lee_ideals_collapsed }
						}
						complete_national_focus = KOR_lee_ideals_collapsed
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_lee_neutral }
						}
						complete_national_focus = KOR_lee_neutral
					}
					country_event = { id = korea.867 days = 35 }
				}
				remove_ideas = KOR_lee_fight_for_taiwan_idea
			}
			KOR = {
				set_rule = { can_send_volunteers = yes }
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = KOR_after_bubble_3_idea
				add_idea = KOR_after_bubble_defeat_idea
			}
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.11.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					KOR_is_yoon_path = yes
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = authoritarian_democrat
					elections_allowed = yes
				}
				KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char = {
					set_portraits = {
						civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_defeat_civilian_large }
					}
					add_country_leader_role = {
						country_leader = {
							desc = KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char_defeat_desc
							ideology = judicial_authoritarianism
						}
						promote_leader = yes
					}
				}
				remove_ideas = KOR_dispatch_to_taiwan_idea
				load_focus_tree = TFR_tree_KOR_yoon_defeat
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					KOR_is_lee_path = yes
				}
				swap_ruler_traits = {
					remove = kor_lee_jaming_2022_traits
					add = kor_lee_jaming_2022_fail_traits
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
					}
					remove_ideas = KOR_lee_load_of_war_idea
				}
				load_focus_tree = TFR_tree_KOR_lee_defeat
			}
			focus_unlock = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				KOR_is_yoon_path = yes
			}
			complete_national_focus = KOR_defeat_free_democracy
			country_event = { id = korea.46 hours = 6 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				KOR_is_lee_path = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
				}
				country_event = { id = korea.46 hours = 6 }
			}
			KOR_dpk_anxious_unlock_effect = yes
			KOR_dpk_set_anxious_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.22 #양안전쟁 승리
	title = korea.22.t
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					KOR_is_lee_path = yes
					has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_lee_path }
			}
		}
		text = korea.22.desc
	}	
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			KOR_is_lee_path = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag }
		}
		text = korea.22.nothing.desc
	}	
	picture = GFX_report_event_kor_china_win
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					CHI = { exists = yes }
				}
				CHI = {
					transfer_state = 1451
					transfer_state = 1150
					transfer_state = 1148
				}
			}
			# 홍준표 대통령: 윤석열 승전 중점·초상화 변경이 적용되지 않게 한다
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_hong_president
				}
				log = "TFRKPHN_DIAG korea.22 hong victory branch"
				# 승전: 전국 지지도 +15
				set_temp_variable = { TFRKPHN_support_delta = 15 }
				TFRKPHN_support_add_all = yes
				country_event = { id = korea.174 days = 15 }
				remove_ideas = KOR_dispatch_to_taiwan_idea
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					KOR_is_yoon_path = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = KOR_yoon_taiwan_war_victory
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_youngsan }
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youngsan
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_economic }
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youn_economic
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_socity }
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youn_socity
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_police_department }
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youn_police_department
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_father
							has_completed_focus = KOR_koreajapan
						}
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youn_father
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_party_control
							has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_populace_first
							has_completed_focus = KOR_youn_party_together
						}
					}
					complete_national_focus = KOR_youn_party_together
				}
				KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char = {
					set_portraits = {
						civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_victory_civilian_large }
					}
					swap_country_leader_traits = {
						remove = kor_yoon_traits
						add = kor_red_dragon_killer
						ideology = judicial_conservatism
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_leader = { character = KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char ruling_only = yes } }
					set_country_leader_description = {
						ideology = conservative
						desc = KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char_victory_desc
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = korea.174 days = 15 }
				country_event = { id = korea.94 days = 7 }
				remove_ideas = KOR_dispatch_to_taiwan_idea
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					KOR_is_lee_path = yes
				}
				set_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
					}
					set_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory_supported
					remove_ideas = KOR_lee_load_of_war_idea
					KOR_lee_jae_myung_char = {
						set_portraits = {
							civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_KOR_lee_jae_myung_char_great_civilian_large }
						}
						swap_country_leader_traits = {
							remove = kor_lee_jaming_2022_traits
							add = kor_lee_jaming_traits
							ideology = legislative_pragmatism
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_leader = { character = KOR_lee_jae_myung_char ruling_only = yes } }
						set_country_leader_description = {
							ideology = social_liberal
							desc = KOR_lee_jae_myung_char_victory_desc
						}
					}
					KOR_dpk_pre_lee_unlock_effect = yes
					KOR_dpk_set_pre_lee_effect = yes
					country_event = { id = korea.174 days = 15 }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = KOR_after_bubble_3_idea
						add_idea = KOR_after_bubble_win_idea
					}
					remove_ideas = KOR_dispatch_to_taiwan_idea
				}
				else = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_lee_neutral }
						}
						complete_national_focus = KOR_lee_neutral
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_completed_focus = KOR_lee_it_network_act_amendment }
						}
						complete_national_focus = KOR_lee_it_network_act_amendment
					}
				}
				remove_ideas = KOR_lee_fight_for_taiwan_idea
			}
			KOR = {
				set_rule = { can_send_volunteers = yes }
			}
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.22.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_hong_president
				}
				remove_ideas = KOR_dispatch_to_taiwan_idea
				custom_effect_tooltip = TFRKPHN_victory_support_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					KOR_is_yoon_path = yes
				}
				remove_ideas = KOR_dispatch_to_taiwan_idea
				load_focus_tree = TFR_tree_KOR_yoon_victory
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					KOR_is_lee_path = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
					}
					remove_ideas = KOR_lee_load_of_war_idea
					load_focus_tree = TFR_tree_KOR_lee_victory_supported
				}
				else = {
					load_focus_tree = TFR_tree_KOR_lee_victory_nothing
				}
			}
			focus_unlock = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # 제20대 대통령 선거
	id = korea.52
	title = korea.52.t
	desc = korea.52.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_kor_election
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = korea.52.a
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_yoon_support > 0.5 }
		}
		# TFRKP Hong Nak: 경선에서 홍준표를 골랐다면 홍준표가 대통령
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_PPP_hong }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_character = KOR_hong_joon_pyo_char } }
				recruit_character = KOR_hong_joon_pyo_char
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = KOR_hong_joon_pyo_char }
				KOR_hong_joon_pyo_char = {
					promote_character = judicial_conservatism
				}
			}
			# 홍준표 중점 트리/지지도 초기화 이벤트를 확실히 호출 (무작위 발생에만 맡기지 않는다)
			log = "TFRKPHN_DIAG korea.52 hong branch: immediate tree load"
			country_event = { id = tfrkphn.10 hours = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char = {
				promote_character = judicial_conservatism
			}
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = conservative
			last_election = "2022.5.9"
			election_frequency = 60
			elections_allowed = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_yoon_path
		clr_country_flag = KOR_president_election_2022
		focus_unlock = yes
		# TFRKP Hong Nak: 선택 즉시 홍준표 중점 트리로 교체
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_PPP_hong }
			hidden_effect = { TFRKPHN_hong_start_effect = yes }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = korea.292 hours = 6 }
			remove_ideas = KOR_candlelight_idea
			KOR_lee_jae_myung_char = {
				promote_character = legislative_pragmatism
			}
			set_temp_variable = { ruling_party_wing_var_temp = token:market_liberal }
			add_ruling_party_wing = yes
		}
		# TFRKP Hong Nak: 홍준표 경로에서는 안철수 국민의당 합당이 없다
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_PPP_hong } }
				country_event = {
					id = korea.354 #안철수 합당
					hours = 12
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.52.b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_lee_support > 0.5 }
		}
		# TFRKP Hong Nak: 경선에서 이낙연을 골랐다면 이낙연이 대통령
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_DPK_lee_nakyeon }
			KOR_lee_nak_yon_char = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					country_leader = {
						ideology = korean_social_liberal
						expire = "2065.1.1"
						id = -1
					}
					promote_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			KOR_lee_jae_myung_char = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					country_leader = {
						desc = KOR_lee_jae_myung_char_2022_desc
						traits = { kor_lee_jaming_2022_traits }
						ideology = korean_social_liberal
					}
					promote_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			focus_unlock = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_lee_path
		clr_country_flag = KOR_president_election_2022
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_DPK_lee_nakyeon } } KOR_lee_jae_myung_char = {
				set_portraits = { 
					civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_KOR_lee_jae_myung_char_2022_civilian_large } 
				}
			}
			}
			set_rule = { can_send_volunteers = no }
			country_event = { id = korea.593 hours = 6 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.52.c
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_yoon_support = 0.5 }
			check_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_lee_support = 0.5 }
		}
		# TFRKP Hong Nak: 경선에서 홍준표를 골랐다면 홍준표가 대통령
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_PPP_hong }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_character = KOR_hong_joon_pyo_char } }
				recruit_character = KOR_hong_joon_pyo_char
			}
			KOR_hong_joon_pyo_char = {
				promote_character = judicial_conservatism
			}
		}
		else = {
			KOR_yoon_suk_yeol_char = {
				promote_character = judicial_conservatism
			}
		}
		KOR_lee_jae_myung_char = {
			promote_character = legislative_pragmatism
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = conservative
			last_election = "2022.5.9"
			election_frequency = 60
			elections_allowed = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = KOR_yoon_path
		clr_country_flag = KOR_president_election_2022
		focus_unlock = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_ideas = KOR_candlelight_idea
			KOR_lee_jae_myung_char = {
				promote_character = legislative_pragmatism
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = market_liberal
				long_name = KOR_market_liberal_party_PPP_long
				name = KOR_market_liberal_party_PPP
			}
			set_temp_variable = { ruling_party_wing_var_temp = token:market_liberal }
			add_ruling_party_wing = yes
		}
		# TFRKP Hong Nak: 홍준표 경로에서는 안철수 국민의당 합당이 없다
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_PPP_hong } }
				country_event = {
					id = korea.354 #안철수 합당
					hours = 12
				}
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			remove_ideas = KOR_civil_unrest1
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # 다크 호스 (TFRKP Hong Nak: 화면에 보이는 이벤트는 tfrkphn.181이 담당. 이 이벤트는 연결만 한다)
	id = korea.81
	hidden = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = TFRKPHN_seen_k81 }
		NOT = { has_global_flag = TFRKPHN_k81_done }
	}

	immediate = {
		log = "TFRKPHN_DIAG korea.81 dispatcher fired"
		# 경선 승리 이벤트는 게임 전체에서 딱 한 번만
		set_global_flag = TFRKPHN_k81_done
		set_country_flag = TFRKPHN_seen_k81
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_PPP_hong }
			country_event = { id = tfrkphn.181 }
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = tfrkphn.182 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # 양안전쟁 승리 후 총선 승리
	id = korea.151
	title = korea.151.t
	desc = korea.151.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_ppp_ge_win

	is_triggered_only = yes

	# TFRKP Hong Nak: 홍준표 참전 승전 경로에서는 tfrkphn.151 이 이 총선 이벤트를 대신한다
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			AND = {
				has_country_flag = TFRKP_HN_hong_president
				has_completed_focus = TFRKPHN_hong_b02_02
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_election_of_NA_2024
			set_country_flag = KOR_election_of_NA_2024_tw_win
			set_country_flag = KOR_yoon_second_election_victory
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.151.a
		set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 121 }
		set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 170 }
		set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 2 }
		set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 5 }
		KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes

		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_NatAsm_changed_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { #이준석과 함께
					has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_lee_friend_flag
				}
				#개혁신당 의석 전부 국힘
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_seat_temp = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_temp = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_target_temp = 4 }
				KOR_change_NatAsm_seats_specific_1 = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { NA_show_LP = 1 } # 개혁신당 등장
			}
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			complete_national_focus = KOR_yoon_ready_election
		}
	}
}

				has_game_rule = {
					rule = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon
					option = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon_2
				}
				factor = 500
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.275.a
		trigger = { has_idea = KOR_ahn_cheol_soo_ppp_ns }
		KOR_kim_jae_sub_char = { promote_character = yoonism }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon
						option = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon_1
					}
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon
						option = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon_2
					}
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon
					option = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon_3
				}
				factor = 500
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.275.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_idea = KOR_choo_kyung_ho_ppp_ns
				has_idea = KOR_kweon_seong_dong_ppp_ns
				has_idea = KOR_joo_ho_young_ppp_ns
				has_idea = KOR_ahn_cheol_soo_ppp_ns
			}
		}
		KOR_cho_kyoung_tae_char = { promote_character = yoonism }
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			add_ideas = {
				KOR_baek_jong_heon_HM
				KOR_kim_sung_han_FM
				KOR_yoon_hee_suk_EM
				KOR_park_wan_su_IM
				KOR_nam_joo_hong_IntelM
				KOR_shin_won_sik_TM
			}
		}
	}
}

## 윤버지!!! ##
# 새로운 캐릭터가 윤버지의 가호를 받아 윤핵관 인물 중 한 명을 대체합니다.
country_event = {
	id = koryhg.1
	title = koryhg.1.t
	desc = koryhg.1.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_yhg_1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_yhg_event_pending_flag
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = koryhg.1.a
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event koryhg.1 option koryhg.1.a"

		KOR_remove_replaced_advisor_1_effects = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt

		set_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_1 = TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor }
		KOR_add_replace_adviosr_effects = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = koryhg.1.b
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event koryhg.1 option koryhg.1.b"

		KOR_remove_replaced_advisor_2_effects = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt

		set_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_2 = TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor }
		KOR_add_replace_adviosr_effects = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = koryhg.1.c
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event koryhg.1 option koryhg.1.c"

		KOR_remove_replaced_advisor_3_effects = yes

		set_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_3 = TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor }
		KOR_add_replace_adviosr_effects = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = koryhg.1.d
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event koryhg.1 option koryhg.1.d"

		hidden_effect = {
			#Cleaning vars / flags / etc
			clear_variable = TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor

			clr_country_flag = TFRKOR_yhg_cd_flag
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			#Cleaning vars / flags / etc
			clear_variable = TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor
			clr_country_flag = KOR_yhg_event_pending_flag
		}
	}
}
# 임명하려는 인물이 윤핵관에 오른 인물과 같은 트레잇을 가진 경우, 플레이어가 선택한 인물이 윤버지의 가호를 받아 윤핵관 인물 중 같은 트레잇을 가진 한 명을 대체합니다.
country_event = {
	id = koryhg.2
	title = koryhg.2.t
	desc = koryhg.2.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_yhg_moj

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_yhg_event_pending_flag
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = koryhg.2.a
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event koryhg.2 option koryhg.2.a"
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_1 = 9 }
					check_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_1 = 2 }
				}
			}
			KOR_remove_replaced_advisor_1_effects = yes

			set_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_1 = TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor }
			KOR_add_replace_adviosr_effects = yes
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_2 = 9 }
					check_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_2 = 2 }
				}
			}
			KOR_remove_replaced_advisor_2_effects = yes

			set_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_2 = TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor }
			KOR_add_replace_adviosr_effects = yes
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_3 = 9 }
					check_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_3 = 2 }
				}
			}
			KOR_remove_replaced_advisor_3_effects = yes

			set_variable = { TFR_KOR_ascended_advisor_3 = TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor }
			KOR_add_replace_adviosr_effects = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = koryhg.2.b
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event koryhg.2 option koryhg.2.b"

		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = TFRKOR_yhg_cd_flag
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			#Cleaning vars / flags / etc
			clear_variable = TFR_KOR_ascending_advisor
			clr_country_flag = KOR_yhg_event_pending_flag
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = koryhg.4
	title = koryhg.4.t
	desc = koryhg.4.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_ppp

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {

	}

	option = {
		name = koryhg.4.a
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event koryhg.4 option koryhg.4.a"
		set_variable = { TFR_KOR_PPP_inner_circle_1 = 2 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = KOR_choo_kyung_ho_ppp_ns }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = KOR_will_continue_party_leader_tooltip
				CHARACTER = KOR_choo_kyung_ho
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = KOR_choo_kyung_ho_ppp_ns
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = KOR_kweon_seong_dong_ppp_ns }
			remove_ideas = KOR_kweon_seong_dong_ppp_ns
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = KOR_joo_ho_young_ppp_ns }
			remove_ideas = KOR_joo_ho_young_ppp_ns
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = KOR_ahn_cheol_soo_ppp_ns }
			remove_ideas = KOR_ahn_cheol_soo_ppp_ns
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon
						option = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon_2
					}
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon
						option = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon_3
					}
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon
					option = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon_1
				}
				factor = 500
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = koryhg.4.b
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event koryhg.4 option koryhg.4.b"
		set_variable = { TFR_KOR_PPP_inner_circle_1 = 3 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = KOR_joo_ho_young_ppp_ns }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = KOR_will_continue_party_leader_tooltip
				CHARACTER = KOR_joo_ho_young
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = KOR_joo_ho_young_ppp_ns
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = KOR_kweon_seong_dong_ppp_ns }
			remove_ideas = KOR_kweon_seong_dong_ppp_ns
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = KOR_choo_kyung_ho_ppp_ns }
			remove_ideas = KOR_choo_kyung_ho_ppp_ns
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = KOR_ahn_cheol_soo_ppp_ns }
			remove_ideas = KOR_ahn_cheol_soo_ppp_ns
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = TFRKP_2032_president_election_yoon

country_event = { #계엄실패 2030년 제22대 대선
	id = korea.351
	title = korea.351.t
	desc = korea.351.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_polite_2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = social_liberal
				election_frequency = 60
				elections_allowed = yes
			}
			KOR_lee_jae_myung_char = {
				remove_country_leader_role = {
					ideology = legislative_pragmatism
				}
			}
			KOR_kim_min_seok_char = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					country_leader = {
						desc = KOR_kim_min_seok_char_desc
						ideology = legislative_pragmatism
					}
					promote_leader = yes
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = KOR_second_president_DP
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.351.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			KOR_kim_min_seok_char = {
				add_country_leader_role = {
					country_leader = {
						desc = KOR_kim_min_seok_char_desc
						ideology = legislative_pragmatism
					}
					promote_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			add_ideas = {
				KOR_yoon_ho_jung_HM
				KOR_wi_sung_lac_FM
				KOR_ha_joon_kyung_EM
				KOR_yeom_tae_young_IM
				KOR_park_sun_won_IntelM
				KOR_kim_byung_joo_TM
			}
		}
	}
}

		set_variable = { NA_show_ORP = 1 } # 우리공화당 등장
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.363.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_election_of_NA_2028 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_seat_temp = 43 }
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_temp = 1 } #민주당
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_target_temp = 2 } #국힘
			KOR_change_NatAsm_seats_specific_1 = yes
			#
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_seat_temp = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_temp = 4 } #국민의당 or 개혁신당
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_target_temp = 2 } #국힘
			KOR_change_NatAsm_seats_specific_1 = yes
		}
	}
}

					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_solo_constitutional_amendment_flag }

						has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_jeong_cheong_rae_nominee_flag

				}
			}

				ruling_party = social_liberal
				elections_allowed = yes

				election_frequency = 48
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = social_liberal
				elections_allowed = yes
				last_election = "[CURRENT_DATE]"
				election_frequency = 60
			}
		}
		KOR_jeong_chung_rae_char = {
			add_country_leader_role = {

					desc = KOR_jeong_chung_rae_char_desc

		}

country_event = {
	id = korea.638
	title = korea.638.title
	desc = korea.638.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_850

	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_flag # 이거 뜨고 새 당대표 트리거 없애기
			set_country_flag = KOR_plp_ongoing_ph_flag
			clr_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_pp
			clr_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_jp
			clr_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_lp
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { KOR_plp_pp_inf_var > KOR_plp_lp_inf_var }
			check_variable = { KOR_plp_pp_inf_var > KOR_plp_jp_inf_var }
		}
		name = korea.638.a
		set_variable = { PLPPartyLeader_var = 1 }
		set_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_pp
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = TFRKP_lee_reds_lead_faction_of_party
					option = TFRKP_lee_reds_lead_faction_of_party_PP
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { # 정의당이 가장 세력이 쎔
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_jp_inf_var > KOR_plp_lp_inf_var }
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_jp_inf_var > KOR_plp_pp_inf_var }
				}				
				# 정의당이 균형의 수호자임
				AND = {
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_jp_inf_var = KOR_plp_lp_inf_var }
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_lp_inf_var > 0 }
				}
				AND = { # JP = PP
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_jp_inf_var = KOR_plp_pp_inf_var }
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_pp_inf_var > 0 }
				}
				AND = { # LP = PP
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_lp_inf_var = KOR_plp_pp_inf_var }
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_pp_inf_var > 0 }
				}
				AND = { # 전부 0
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_jp_inf_var = KOR_plp_pp_inf_var }
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_lp_inf_var = KOR_plp_pp_inf_var }
					check_variable = { KOR_plp_pp_inf_var = 0 }
				}
			}
		}
		name = korea.638.b
		set_variable = { PLPPartyLeader_var = 8 }
		set_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_jp
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = TFRKP_lee_reds_lead_faction_of_party
					option = TFRKP_lee_reds_lead_faction_of_party_JP
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { KOR_plp_lp_inf_var > KOR_plp_jp_inf_var }
			check_variable = { KOR_plp_lp_inf_var > KOR_plp_pp_inf_var }
		}
		name = korea.638.c
		set_variable = { PLPPartyLeader_var = 9 }
		set_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_lp
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = TFRKP_lee_reds_lead_faction_of_party
					option = TFRKP_lee_reds_lead_faction_of_party_LP
				}
			}
		}
	}
	after = {	
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_plp_president_22th_is_tt
		clr_country_flag = KOR_plp_ongoing_ph_flag
	}
}

				}

			KOR = { puppet = KPA }

				KOR_add_to_pdto_effect = yes

	}

			add_country_leader_role = {

					desc = KOR_yoon_jong_o_char_desc

					ideology = progressive_democracy
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_ideas = {
				KOR_song_myung_sook_HM
				KOR_lee_hae_young_FM
				KOR_na_won_joon_EM
				KOR_kang_sung_hee_IM

		}

		add_ruling_party_wing = yes

			add_country_leader_role = {

			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_democrat
				popularity = KOR_social_democrat_pop
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_democrat

			}

			set_temp_variable = { KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop = party_popularity@libertarian_socialist }
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop = 0.4 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = libertarian_socialist
				popularity = KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop
			}

			set_temp_variable = { KOR_communist_pop = party_popularity@communist }
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_communist_pop = 0.4 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_communist_pop
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_communist_pop = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = communist
				popularity = KOR_communist_pop
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = KOR_people_democracy_idea
				add_idea = KOR_candlelight_idea
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = korea.847.f
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_pp
				has_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_jp
				has_country_flag = KOR_plp_ph_4th_lp
				has_country_flag = KOR_PLP_DPK_WIN_2028 
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TOOLTIP_KOREA_847_ERROR
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		set_country_flag = KOR_PP_DPK_route
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = social_liberal
			elections_allowed = yes
		}
		KOR_park_yong_jin_char = {
			add_country_leader_role	= {
				country_leader = {
					desc = KOR_park_yong_jin_char_desc
					traits = { pyj_dlp_trait }
					ideology = korean_social_liberal
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KOR_PLP_party_setup_flag
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_social_democrat_pop = party_popularity@social_democrat }
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_social_democrat_pop = 0.2 }
			add_ideas = {
				KOR_han_min_soo_HM
				KOR_cho_sei_young_FM
				KOR_jin_sung_joon_EM
				KOR_lee_hae_sik_IM
				KOR_youn_kun_young_IntelM
				KOR_an_gyu_baek_TM
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_social_democrat_pop
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_social_democrat_pop = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_democrat
				popularity = KOR_social_democrat_pop
			}

			set_temp_variable = { KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop = party_popularity@libertarian_socialist }
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop = 0.4 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = libertarian_socialist
				popularity = KOR_libertarian_socialist_pop
			}

			set_temp_variable = { KOR_communist_pop = party_popularity@communist }
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_communist_pop = 0.4 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_communist_pop
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_communist_pop = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = communist
				popularity = KOR_communist_pop
			}
		}		
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = korea.941
	title = korea.941.title
	desc = korea.941.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_941

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = korea.941.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_nothing_2027_dpk_fail_flag
				check_variable = { KOR_2027_ahn_ppp_united_var > 49 }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_event_korea_941_a
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support > 0.5 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support_temp = 0.10 }
			KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support_change_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support = 0.56 }
			set_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support = 0.44 }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = korea.623 days = 1 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = korea.941.b
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { KOR_2027_ahn_ppp_united_var < 50 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_lee_nothing_2027_dpk_fail_flag }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_event_korea_941_b
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support > 0.5 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support_temp = 0.10 }
			KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support_change_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_2027_lee_support = 0.48 }
			set_variable = { KOR.KOR_president_election_2027_cho_support = 0.52 }
		}
	}
}

		set_country_flag = KOR_lee_dpk_cho_korea_flag
		effect_tooltip = {
			focus_unlock = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = KOR_cho_kuk_korea

				ideology = authoritarian_democrat
				popularity = -1
			}

				ideology = conservative
				popularity = KOR_authoritarian_democrat_pop
			}
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_social_liberal_pop = party_popularity@social_liberal }
			set_temp_variable = { KOR_social_liberal_second_pop = party_popularity@social_liberal }
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_social_liberal_pop = 0.8 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_social_liberal_second_pop = 0.2 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = authoritarian_democrat
				popularity = KOR_social_liberal_pop
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_social_liberal_pop = -1 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_social_liberal_pop
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberal
				popularity = KOR_social_liberal_second_pop
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = authoritarian_democrat
				popularity = 0.08
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = conservative
				popularity = -0.08
			}
			KOR_cho_guk_char = {
				swap_country_leader_traits = {
					remove = kor_cho_guk_traits
					add = kor_cho_guk_father_traits
					ideology = reformical_democracy
				}

add_namespace = kimminseok_korea_war

############################################################
# .1 도입: 북한, 동해로 미사일 발사 → 김민석의 결단
#    (김민석 루트 진입 시점의 이펙트에서 country_event = kimminseok_korea_war.1 로 호출)
############################################################
country_event = {
	id = kimminseok_korea_war.1
	title = kimminseok_korea_war.1.t
	desc = kimminseok_korea_war.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_kimminseok_korea_war_missile

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {	# "늘 식민주의만 겪었다. 공격적인 관점을 가질 때가 됐다."
		name = kimminseok_korea_war.1.a
		kimminseok_korea_war_init = yes
		set_country_flag = kimminseok_korea_war_unlocked
		custom_effect_tooltip = kimminseok_korea_war_UNLOCK_TT
	}
}

############################################################
# .2 북한의 반응 - 긴장 고조, 60일 카운트다운 개시
############################################################
country_event = {
	id = kimminseok_korea_war.2
	title = kimminseok_korea_war.2.t
	desc = kimminseok_korea_war.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_kimminseok_korea_war_response

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = kimminseok_korea_war.2.a
		add_to_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_tension = 10 }
		kimminseok_korea_war_update_ui = yes
		set_country_flag = kimminseok_korea_war_countdown_active
		custom_effect_tooltip = kimminseok_korea_war_COUNTDOWN_START_TT
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = korea.1327 days = 3 }
		}
		country_event = { id = kimminseok_korea_war.10 days = 1 }	# 카운트다운 루프 시작
	}
}

############################################################
# .10 [숨김] 60일 카운트다운 루프 (매일 자기 자신 재예약)
############################################################
country_event = {
	id = kimminseok_korea_war.10
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = kimminseok_korea_war_countdown_active }
			subtract_from_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_deadline_days = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_dirty = 1 }

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { kimminseok_korea_war_deadline_days > 0 } }
				country_event = { id = kimminseok_korea_war.10 days = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				# 시간 초과 → 실패 (툴팁 전용 디버프, 체인은 계속 진행)
				clr_country_flag = kimminseok_korea_war_countdown_active
				set_country_flag = kimminseok_korea_war_prep_failed
				country_event = { id = kimminseok_korea_war.40 }
			}
		}
	}
}

		clr_country_flag = TFRKP_regiondev_enabled_flag

add_namespace = kor_countdown

country_event = {
	id = kor_countdown.1
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		# 취소된 카운트다운의 잔여 이벤트가 실행되는 것 방지
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_const_countdown_active
			 }

			subtract_from_variable = { KOR_regiondev_days_fake_var = 1 }

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { KOR_regiondev_days_fake_var > 0 } has_country_flag = KOR_const_countdown_active}
				country_event = { id = kor_countdown.1 days = 1 }   # 내일 다시 자기 자신 호출
			}
		}
	}
}
country_event = {
	id = kor_countdown.2
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		# 취소된 카운트다운의 잔여 이벤트가 실행되는 것 방지
		if = {
			limit = {
					tag = KOR
					has_country_flag = TFRKP_regiondev_enabled_flag
				}
				# Resolve the campaign when the existing 2028 election event fires.
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_lee_2028_election_flag
						NOT = { has_country_flag = TFRKP_regiondev_election_done_flag }
					}
					TFRKP_regiondev_election_resolve_effect = yes
				}
				country_event = { id = kor_countdown.2 days = 1 }   # 내일 다시 자기 자신 호출
				TFRKP_regiondev_daily_tick_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.1026
	title = korea.1026.title
	desc = korea.1026.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_1026

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = korea.1026.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = korea.1026.tt
		news_event = { id = kor_news.73 }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					PD1 = {
						exists = no
					}
				}
				KOR = {
					puppet = PD1
					if = {
						limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
						add_to_faction = PD1
					}
				}
			}
			PD1 = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = PD1_manchuria_social_correction_pilot_zone
				set_country_flag = PD1_manchuria_social_correction_pilot_zone_established_v1

				set_party_name = {
					ideology = authoritarian_democrat
					long_name = PD1_authoritarian_democrat_party_manchuria_social_correction_bureau_long
					name = PD1_authoritarian_democrat_party_manchuria_social_correction_bureau
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character = PD1_tae_yeongho_char
						NOT = { has_country_flag = PD1_tae_yeongho_chogukism_overlap_removed_v1 }
					}
					PD1_tae_yeongho_char = {
						remove_country_leader_role = {
							ideology = ultra_conservatism
						}
					}
					set_country_flag = PD1_tae_yeongho_chogukism_overlap_removed_v1
				}
				# Save migration from the former social-liberal version.
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = PD1_yoo_si_min_leader_role_added_v1
					}
					PD1_yoo_si_min_char = {
						remove_country_leader_role = {
							ideology = centrist
						}
					}
					clr_country_flag = PD1_yoo_si_min_leader_role_added_v1
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = PD1_yoo_si_min_chogukism_leader_role_added_v1 }
					}
					add_country_leader_role = {
						character = PD1_yoo_si_min_char
						promote_leader = yes
						country_leader = {
							desc = PD1_yoo_si_min_char_desc
							ideology = chogukism
							traits = { PD1_first_citizen_trait }
						}
					}
					set_country_flag = PD1_yoo_si_min_chogukism_leader_role_added_v1
				}
				promote_character = {
					character = PD1_yoo_si_min_char
					ideology = chogukism
				}
				set_popularities = {
					authoritarian_democrat = 100
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = authoritarian_democrat
					elections_allowed = no
				}
			}
			610 = {
				add_claim_by = PD1
			}
			country_event = { id = korea.1370 days = 2 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.1029
	title = korea.1029.title
	desc = korea.1029.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_1029

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = korea.1029.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = korea.1029.tt
		news_event = { id = kor_news.74 }
		hidden_effect = {
			KOR = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction = yes
					}
					leave_faction = yes
				}
				create_faction = KOR_west_pacific_security_conference_faction
				clr_country_flag = pdto_member
				set_country_flag = KOR_west_pacific_security_conference_member
				hidden_effect = {
					remove_faction_goal = faction_goal_the_atomic_race
					add_faction_goal = faction_goal_placeholder_long_term
					remove_faction_goal = faction_goal_a_military_base
					add_faction_goal = faction_goal_placeholder_medium_term
					add_faction_goal = faction_goal_placeholder_short_term
				}

				# Move the puppet governments created through korea.1026 into the new faction.
				if = {
					limit = {
						PD1 = {
							exists = yes
							is_puppet_of = KOR
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							PD1 = {
								NOT = { is_in_faction_with = KOR }
							}
						}
						PD1 = {
							if = {
								limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
								leave_faction = yes
							}
						}
						add_to_faction = PD1
					}
					PD1 = {
						clr_country_flag = pdto_member
						clr_country_flag = csto_member
						set_country_flag = KOR_west_pacific_security_conference_member
					}
				}

				# If PD4 was entrusted to Mongolia, move Mongolia before its puppet.
				if = {
					limit = {
						MON = { exists = yes }
						PD4 = {
							exists = yes
							is_puppet_of = MON
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							MON = {
								NOT = { is_in_faction_with = KOR }
							}
						}
						MON = {
							if = {
								limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
								leave_faction = yes
							}
						}
						add_to_faction = MON
					}
					MON = {
						clr_country_flag = pdto_member
						clr_country_flag = csto_member
						set_country_flag = KOR_west_pacific_security_conference_member
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						PD4 = {
							exists = yes
							OR = {
								is_puppet_of = KOR
								is_puppet_of = MON
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							PD4 = {
								NOT = { is_in_faction_with = KOR }
							}
						}
						PD4 = {
							if = {
								limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
								leave_faction = yes
							}
						}
						add_to_faction = PD4
					}
					PD4 = {
						clr_country_flag = pdto_member
						clr_country_flag = csto_member
						set_country_flag = KOR_west_pacific_security_conference_member
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						PD5 = {
							exists = yes
							is_puppet_of = KOR
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							PD5 = {
								NOT = { is_in_faction_with = KOR }
							}
						}
						PD5 = {
							if = {
								limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
								leave_faction = yes
							}
						}
						add_to_faction = PD5
					}
					PD5 = {
						clr_country_flag = pdto_member
						clr_country_flag = csto_member
						set_country_flag = KOR_west_pacific_security_conference_member
					}
				}
			}
			## 다롄 반환
			transfer_state = 1394
			1394 = { set_compliance = 50 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.1132
	title = korea.1132.title
	desc = korea.1132.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_1132

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = korea.1132.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_yeoya
					check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 159 }
				}
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_no_yeoya
					check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 194 }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_yes_votes_var = KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_yeoya
				}
				add_to_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_yes_votes_var = 45 }
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_solo_constitutional_amendment_flag
				add_to_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_yes_votes_var = 6 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_no_votes_var = 298 }
			subtract_from_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_no_votes_var = KOR_LJS_constitution_yes_votes_var }
			country_event = korea.1133
		}

	}
	option = {
		name = korea.1132.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_yeoya
					NOT = { check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 159 } }
				}
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_no_yeoya
					NOT = { check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 194 } }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_yes_votes_var = KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_yeoya
				}
				add_to_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_yes_votes_var = 34 }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_yes_votes_var = 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_no_votes_var = 286 }
			subtract_from_variable = { KOR_LJS_constitution_no_votes_var = KOR_LJS_constitution_yes_votes_var }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_pivot_sejong_capital_promise_done
				}
				country_event = korea.1335
			}
			else = {
				country_event = korea.1137
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.1351
	title = korea.1351.title
	desc = korea.1351.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_1351

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = korea.1351.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_LJS_portrait_happy_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = KOR_LJS_wartime_government_phase_flag
			set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_post_1351_phase_flag
			KOR_lee_jun_seok_char = {
				set_portraits = {
					civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_KOR_lee_jun_seok_char_happy_civilian_large }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_flag }
				KOR_lee_jun_seok_char = {
					swap_country_leader_traits = {
						remove = kor_lee_jun_seok_wandering_boy_traits
						add = kor_lee_jun_seok_responsible_adult_traits
						ideology = korean_conservatism
					}
					swap_country_leader_traits = {
						remove = kor_lee_jun_seok_wandering_boy_traits
						add = kor_lee_jun_seok_responsible_adult_traits
						ideology = sextonism
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				KOR_lee_jun_seok_char = {
					swap_country_leader_traits = {
						remove = kor_lee_jun_seok_traits
						add = kor_lee_jun_seok_responsible_adult_traits
						ideology = korean_conservatism
					}
					swap_country_leader_traits = {
						remove = kor_lee_jun_seok_traits
						add = kor_lee_jun_seok_responsible_adult_traits
						ideology = sextonism
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_leader = { character = KOR_lee_jun_seok_char ruling_only = yes } }
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_leader_ideology = sextonism }
					set_country_leader_description = {
						ideology = market_liberal
						desc = KOR_lee_jun_seok_char_responsible_adult_desc
					}
				}
				else = {
					set_country_leader_description = {
						ideology = conservative
						desc = KOR_lee_jun_seok_char_responsible_adult_desc
					}
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = korea.1116 days = 18 }
			country_event = { id = korea.1130 days = 57 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_flag
						has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_solo_constitutional_amendment_flag
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = korea.1113 days = 101 }
				country_event = { id = korea.695 days = 190 }
				country_event = { id = korea.696 days = 236 }
				country_event = { id = korea.702 days = 285 }
			}
			country_event = { id = korea.1147 days = 335 }
		}
	}
}

	title = korea.1352.title
	desc = korea.1352.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_1352

	trigger = {
		tag = KOR
		KOR_is_lee_PPP_path = yes
		has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_government_enable_flag
		date > 2029.2.15
	}

	fire_only_once = yes

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.1352.a
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.1358
	title = korea.1358.title
	desc = korea.1358.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_1358

	trigger = {
		tag = KOR
		KOR_is_lee_PPP_path = yes
		has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_government_enable_flag
		date > 2029.6.28
		NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_great_asia_war }
	}
	fire_only_once = yes
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

	option = {
		name = korea.1358.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = korea.1358.tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_LJS_portrait_sad_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_flag
			KOR_lee_jun_seok_char = {
				set_portraits = {
					civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_KOR_lee_jun_seok_char_sad_civilian_large }
				}
				swap_country_leader_traits = {
					remove = kor_lee_jun_seok_traits
					add = kor_lee_jun_seok_wandering_boy_traits
					ideology = korean_conservatism
				}
				swap_country_leader_traits = {
					remove = kor_lee_jun_seok_traits
					add = kor_lee_jun_seok_wandering_boy_traits
					ideology = sextonism
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_leader = { character = KOR_lee_jun_seok_char ruling_only = yes } }
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_leader_ideology = sextonism }
					set_country_leader_description = {
						ideology = market_liberal
						desc = KOR_lee_jun_seok_char_wandering_boy_desc
					}
				}
				else = {
					set_country_leader_description = {
						ideology = conservative
						desc = KOR_lee_jun_seok_char_wandering_boy_desc
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.1362
	title = korea.1362.title
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 200 }
		}
		text = korea.1362.landslide.desc
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 150 }
			NOT = { check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 200 } }
		}
		text = korea.1362.majority.desc
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 150 } }
		}
		text = korea.1362.minority.desc
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_1362

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = korea.1362.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_NatAsm_changed_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 200 }
			}
			add_political_power = 100
			add_stability = 0.04
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats > 150 }
			}
			add_political_power = 50
			add_stability = 0.02
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_LJS_portrait_GG_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			add_political_power = -50
			add_stability = -0.03
			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = korea.1137.tt.2
			hidden_effect = {
				KOR_lee_jun_seok_char = {
					set_portraits = {
						civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_KOR_lee_jun_seok_char_GG_civilian_large }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea.1364
	title = korea.1364.title
	desc = korea.1364.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_1364

	trigger = {
		tag = KOR
		KOR_is_lee_PPP_path = yes
		has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_government_enable_flag
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_flag
			date > 2029.6.28
		}
		has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_choice_pending_flag
		NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_post_1351_phase_flag }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_great_asia_war }
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = korea.1364.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_LJS_event_1364_a_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_momemtum_var
				value = KOR_LJS_late_term_momentum_reward_var
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_LJS_late_term_momentum_reward_var = 0 }
			clr_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_choice_pending_flag
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = korea.1364.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_LJS_event_1364_b_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				25 = { add_to_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_newness_army_attack_factor_dynamic value = 0.01 } }
				25 = { add_to_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_newness_army_defence_factor_dynamic value = 0.01 } }
				25 = { add_to_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_newness_army_org_factor_dynamic value = 0.01 } }
				25 = { add_to_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_newness_planning_speed_dynamic value = 0.01 } }
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_LJS_late_term_momentum_reward_var = 0 }
			clr_country_flag = KOR_LJS_late_term_choice_pending_flag
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea_hidden.205
	title = korea_hidden.205.t
	desc = korea_hidden.205.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_fall_pyongyang
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	option = {
		name = korea_hidden.205.a
		KOR_NatAsm_remove_speaker_effect = yes
		clr_country_flag = speaker_vacant_2024
		###
		if = {
			limit = { #빛버지
				has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_second_election_victory
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_jeong_jin_seok
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { #계엄버지 - 이재명
				has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_taiwan_war_defeat
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_woo_won_shik
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 빛재명
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_woo_won_shik
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag }
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_seol_hoon
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_defeat
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_joo_ho_yeong
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { KOR_is_yoon_declare_martial_law = yes }
			}
			country_event = { id = korea_hidden.206 days = 723 } #22대 하반기
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea_hidden.207
	title = korea_hidden.207.t
	desc = korea_hidden.207.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_fall_pyongyang
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	option = {
		name = korea_hidden.207.a
		KOR_NatAsm_remove_speaker_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = KOR_election_of_NA_2028
		if = {
			limit = { #빛버지
				has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_second_election_victory
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_kwon_yeong_se

			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 128 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 169 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 2 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 0 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 1 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
			set_country_flag = KOR_election_of_NA_2028_tw_win
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { #계엄버지 - 이재명
				has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_taiwan_war_defeat
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_song_yeong_gil
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = PPP_was_dismentled
				}
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 218 } #민주당계
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 56 } # 민정당계
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 9 } #진보 5 기소 1 사민 1 정의 1 녹색 1 노동 1 민민 1
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 8 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 9 }
				KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
				if = {
					limit = { #이준석과 함께
						has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_lee_friend_flag
					}
					#개혁신당 의석 전부 민주당
					set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_seat_temp = 7 }
					set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_temp = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_target_temp = 4 }
					KOR_change_NatAsm_seats_specific_1 = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 195 } #민주당계
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 83 } # 민정당계
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 10 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 9 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 3 }
				KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
				if = {
					limit = { #이준석과 함께
						has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_lee_friend_flag
					}
					#개혁신당 의석 전부 민주당
					set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_seat_temp = 8 }
					set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_temp = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_target_temp = 4 }
					KOR_change_NatAsm_seats_specific_1 = yes
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = KOR_election_of_NA_2028_mtl
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 빛재명
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_song_yeong_gil

			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 201 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 96 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 1 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 2 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
			TFRKP_regiondev_election_resolve_effect = yes
			country_event = korea.1320
			set_variable = { DPKPartyLeader_var = 1 }
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 조국
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_DPK_path
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_hong_yeong_pyo

			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 151 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 145 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 1 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 3 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
			if = {
				limit = { # 민노당 -해산됨-
					has_country_flag = KOR_dlp_dismentled
				}
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_seat_temp = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_temp = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_target_temp = 3 }
				KOR_change_NatAsm_seats_specific_1 = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 이준석
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_PPP_path
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = KOR_yoon_sang_hyun_IntelM
				}
				set_country_flag = speaker_ha_tae_gyung
			}	
			else = {
				set_country_flag = speaker_yoon_sang_hyeon
			}
			KOR_LJS_election_2028 = yes
			set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_election_2028_done_flag
			set_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitution_deadline_active_flag
			activate_mission = LJS_constitution_deadline_mission

			set_variable = {
				var = KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats
				value = KOR_LJS_election_DPK_var
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats
				value = KOR_LJS_election_ppp_var
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats
				value = KOR_LJS_election_minno_var
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 5 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
			set_variable = { NA_show_LUP = 1 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
			country_event = { id = korea.1362 hours = 6 }
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 진보당
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_progress_party_route
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					power_balance_value = {
						id = KOR_bop_plp_NA_fight_balance
						value < 0
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = speaker_sim_sang_jung
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 86 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 55 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 154 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 4 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 1 }
				KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
				custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_NatAsm_changed_tt
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = speaker_lee_in_yeong
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 116 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 105 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 74 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 4 }
				set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 1 }
				KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
				custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_NatAsm_changed_tt
				set_country_flag = KOR_PLP_DPK_WIN_2028
			}
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 거국내각
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_coalition_route
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_kim_ki_hyeon

			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 125 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 133 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 28 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 14 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { KOR_is_yoon_declare_martial_law = yes }
			}
			country_event = { id = korea_hidden.208 days = 723 } #23대 하반기
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = korea_hidden.208
	title = korea_hidden.208.t
	desc = korea_hidden.208.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_fall_pyongyang
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	option = {
		name = korea_hidden.208.a
		KOR_NatAsm_remove_speaker_effect = yes
		###
		if = {
			limit = { #빛버지
				has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_second_election_victory
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_kim_hak_yong
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { #계엄버지 - 이재명
				has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_taiwan_war_defeat
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_lee_in_yeong
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 빛재명
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_lee_in_yeong
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 조국
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_DPK_path
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_roh_woon_rae
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 이준석
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_PPP_path
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_yu_eui_dong
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = KOR_yu_eui_dong_IM
				}	
				add_ideas = KOR_kim_yong_tae_IM
			}
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 진보당
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_progress_party_route
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_PLP_DPK_WIN_2028 }
				}
				set_country_flag = speaker_kim_seon_dong
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = speaker_kim_tae_nyeon
			}
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 거국내각
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_coalition_route
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_an_kyu_baek
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { KOR_is_yoon_declare_martial_law = yes }
			}
			country_event = { id = korea_hidden.209 days = 723 } #24대 상반기
		}
	}
}

			set_country_flag = speaker_jeong_seong_ho
			
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 174 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 116 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 3 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 7 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 조국 "조국의 시간"
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_DPK_path
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_dpk_cho_korea_flag
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_jeon_byung_heon
			
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 163 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 131 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 5 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 1 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
			if = {
				limit = { # 민노당 -해산됨-
					has_country_flag = KOR_dlp_dismentled
				}
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_seat_temp = 5 }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_temp = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_target_temp = 3 }
				KOR_change_NatAsm_seats_specific_1 = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 조국 "실패"
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_DPK_path
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_lee_dpk_cho_korea_flag }
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_kim_ki_hyeon
			
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 145 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 149 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 5 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 1 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
			if = {
				limit = { # 민노당 -해산됨-
					has_country_flag = KOR_dlp_dismentled
				}
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_seat_temp = 5 }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_temp = 2 }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_faction_target_temp = 3 }
				KOR_change_NatAsm_seats_specific_1 = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 이준석
			KOR_LJS_election_2028 = yes
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_PPP_path
			}
			if = { # 해피엔딩
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_lee_PPP_path
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_wartime_bad_ending_flag }
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_amendment_passed_flag
					OR = {
						AND = {
							NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_solo_constitutional_amendment_flag }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_jeong_cheong_rae_nominee_flag }
							has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_flag
							check_variable = { KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_visible > 50 }
						}
						AND = {
							has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_solo_constitutional_amendment_flag
							check_variable = { KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_visible > 60 }
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = speaker_ahn_cheol_soo
				add_to_variable = {
					var = KOR_LJS_election_DPK_var
					value = -10
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					var = KOR_LJS_election_ppp_var
					value = 10
				}
			}
			else_if = { # 정청래 엔딩
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_lee_PPP_path
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_wartime_bad_ending_flag
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_amendment_passed_flag }
						AND = {
							has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_solo_constitutional_amendment_flag
							NOT = { check_variable = { KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_visible > 60 } }
						}
						AND = {
							NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_solo_constitutional_amendment_flag }
							OR = {
								has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_jeong_cheong_rae_nominee_flag
								NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_flag }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = speaker_jeon_byung_heon
				add_to_variable = {
					var = KOR_LJS_election_DPK_var
					value = 20
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					var = KOR_LJS_election_ppp_var
					value = -20
				}
			}
			else = { # 노멀 엔딩
				set_country_flag = speaker_jeong_seong_ho
			}
			clamp_variable = { var = KOR_LJS_election_ppp_var min = 90 max = 220 }
			set_variable = { KOR_LJS_election_DPK_var = 295 }
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_election_DPK_var
				value = KOR_LJS_election_minno_var
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = KOR_LJS_election_DPK_var
				value = KOR_LJS_election_ppp_var
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats
				value = KOR_LJS_election_DPK_var
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats
				value = KOR_LJS_election_ppp_var
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats
				value = KOR_LJS_election_minno_var
			}
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 5 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 진보정당 정의당 엔딩
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_progress_party_route
				has_country_flag = KOR_PP_JP_route
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_kim_jong_hoon
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 진보정당 노동당 엔딩
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_progress_party_route
				has_country_flag = KOR_PP_LP_route
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_kim_jong_hoon
			set_country_flag = KOR_NatAsm_460_seats_flag
			set_variable = { NA_show_KSDLWP = 1 }

			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 165 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 10 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 280 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 0 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 5 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_NatAsm_changed_tt
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 진보정당 기성정당 엔딩
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_progress_party_route
				has_country_flag = KOR_PP_DPK_route
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_yoon_ho_jung
			clr_country_flag = KOR_NatAsm_460_seats_flag
			set_variable = { NA_show_KSDLWP = 1 }

			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 107 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 98 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 93 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 0 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 2 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_NatAsm_changed_tt
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 진보정당 진보당 엔딩
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_progress_party_route
				has_country_flag = KOR_PP_PP_route
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_kim_jong_hoon
			clr_country_flag = KOR_NatAsm_460_seats_flag
			set_variable = { NA_show_KSDLWP = 0 }

			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 106 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 9 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 180 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 0 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 5 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_NatAsm_changed_tt
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 거국내각
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_coalition_route
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_coa_setting_end_flag
					has_country_flag = KOR_coa_minister_im_flag
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_dlp_dismentled }
				}
				set_country_flag = speaker_yoon_ho_jung
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = speaker_na_kyung_won
			}
			
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_DP_seats = 128 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PPP_seats = 139 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_JP_seats = 21 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_PP_seats = 12 }
			set_variable = { KOR_NatAsm_Ind_seats = 0 }
			KOR_rerender_NatAsm = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { KOR_is_yoon_declare_martial_law = yes }
			}
			country_event = { id = korea_hidden.210 days = 723 } #24대 하반기
		}
	}
}

# 11. 24대 하반기
# ===============================
country_event = {
	id = korea_hidden.210
	title = korea_hidden.210.t
	desc = korea_hidden.210.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_210
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	option = {
		name = korea_hidden.210.a
		KOR_NatAsm_remove_speaker_effect = yes
		###
		if = {
			limit = { #빛버지
				has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_second_election_victory
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_ha_tae_gyung
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { #계엄버지 - 이재명
				has_country_flag = KOR_yoon_taiwan_war_defeat
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_yoon_ho_jung
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 빛재명
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_yoon_ho_jung
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 조국 "조국의 시간"
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_DPK_path
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_dpk_cho_korea_flag
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_kim_jong_min
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 조국 "실패"
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_DPK_path
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_lee_dpk_cho_korea_flag }
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_ha_tae_gyung
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 꽁승재명 이준석
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_PPP_path
			}
			if = { # 해피엔딩
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_lee_PPP_path
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_wartime_bad_ending_flag }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_2032_jeong_cheong_rae_nominee_flag }
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_bipartisan_cooperation_flag
					has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_amendment_passed_flag
					check_variable = { KOR_LJS_approval_rating_var_visible > 50 }
				}
				set_country_flag = speaker_kim_se_yeon
			}
			else_if = { # 개헌 실패
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_LJS_constitutional_amendment_passed_flag }
				}
				set_country_flag = speaker_kim_jong_min
			}
			else = { # 노멀 엔딩
				set_country_flag = speaker_yoon_ho_jung
			}
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 진보당
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_progress_party_route
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_PP_DPK_route } 
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_kang_eun_mi
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 진보당 민주당승
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_progress_party_route
				has_country_flag = KOR_PP_DPK_route
			}
			set_country_flag = speaker_lee_hak_yeong
		}
		else_if = { #이재명 - 패전재명 거국내각
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_lee_coalition_route
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KOR_coa_setting_end_flag
					has_country_flag = KOR_coa_minister_im_flag
					NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_dlp_dismentled }
				}
				set_country_flag = speaker_lee_hak_yeong
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = speaker_min_hong_cheol
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # 2024
	id = korea_hidden.230
	
	fire_only_once = yes
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		tag = KOR
		date > 2024.8.17
		has_country_flag = KOR_lee_taiwan_war_victory
		has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag 
	}
	

	option = {
		name = korea_hidden.230.a # 양안전 승
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag
		}
		set_variable = { DPKPartyLeader_var = 1 } # 박찬대
		country_event = { id = korea_hidden.231 days = 730 }
	}
	option = {
		name = korea_hidden.230.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_lee_has_intervened_in_taiwan_flag }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = korea_hidden.300
	title = korea_hidden.300.t
	desc = korea_hidden.300.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_fall_pyongyang

	trigger = {
		tag = KOR
		date > 2020.12.1
		date < 2021.1.30
	}

	fire_only_once = yes

	hidden = yes

	option = {
		name = korea_hidden.300.a
		KOR = { country_event = { id = korea.201 } }
	}
}
